Tervezési csomag · v1.0
MIBOLT / ANGULAR MVP / 2026. 09. 14.

Egy webshop.
Végigtervezve.

Mobiltelefon-választásból végigjárható demórendelés. Egyetlen Angular alkalmazás, valódi API-határral — működő backend nélkül.

Kattintható látványterv ↗
4képernyőcsalád
3layout
44komponensszerződés
30 + 1alap + opcionális feladat
Kötelező minden végrehajtásnál és ellenőrzésnél: mi-bolt-design.

Nem ajánlás. Minden feladatnak külön megvalósítási és ellenőrzési bizonyítékot kell rögzítenie a skill alapján. A vizuálisan nem releváns szabályoknál indokolt „nem alkalmazható” értékelés kell; a skill betöltése ott sem hagyható ki.

Az MVP határa

Ami bekerül

16 fiktív telefon, keresés és szűrés, konkrét szín/tárhely variáns, képgaléria és nagyítás, kosár, vendég checkout, két átvételi és két szimulált fizetési mód, visszakereshető demórendelés.

Angular 22.1.6 az ellenőrzött tervezési alap. A végrehajtáskor újra fel kell oldani a legújabb stabil 22.x verziót, majd pontosan rögzíteni. [S01]

Ami nem kerül be

Backend, SSR, login, előfizetés, részletfizetés, biztosítás, CMS, ügyfélfiók, analitika, social, chat, marketing, valós fizetés, e-mail és szállítás.

A 3D nézegető külön opcionális feladat, nem rejtett alapkövetelmény és nem előfeltétele a működő vásárlásnak.

Három pontosítás, ami védi a tervet

„Rendelni lehessen”: az MSW saját állapotot validál és módosít, készletet kezel, idempotens tranzakciót és demóazonosítót ad. Ez nem valódi kereskedelmi megrendelés.

„E2E”: nincs e2e-csomag a termékgazda 2026-09-20-i döntése óta; a megjelenő felületeket a valós böngészős ellenőrzés fedi le (lásd docs/spec/testing.md).

„Négy képernyő”: főoldal, terméklista, termékoldal és vásárlási képernyőcsalád. A kosár és az öt pénztári állapot nem marad ki a négyes felosztás miatt.

MiBolt MVP · projekt-dokumentáció · 31 feladat / 44 komponens / 16 API-művelet
01 / VIZUÁLIS ÉS INTERAKCIÓS TERV

Négy képernyő.
Egy összefüggő vásárlás.

Desktop és mobil tervek a valódi, kattintható HTML-prototípusból. A prototípus külön állapotválasztót is tartalmaz a hibák és üres állapotok áttekintéséhez.

Az előnézet saját területén görgethető; a nagyítás az eredeti képarányt őrzi. Ezek tervezési képernyőképek, nem a még megépítendő Angular alkalmazás bizonyítékai.

MarketingLayout

S1 · Főoldal

/

Sötét hero, élő cím és CTA, termékillusztráció, három gyors szűrés, négy kiemelt termék, háromlépéses demómagyarázat.

Mobilon a tartalom valóban átrendeződik: előbb az üzenet és CTA, utána az illusztráció és egymás alatti kártyák.

ShopLayout

S2 · Terméklista

/telefonok

264 px oldalsó szűrő, rendezés, találatszám, 12/24-es lapozás. A szűrők kombinációjára illeszkedő variáns ára jelenik meg, nem egy olcsóbb, oda nem illő konfigurációé.

Mobilon alkalmazható vagy elvethető szűrőpanel; külön betöltés, nincs találat, hiba és újrapróbálás.

ShopLayout

S3 · Termékoldal

/telefonok/:slug?variant=:id

7/5-ös galéria–vásárlás arány, valódi elő-/hátulnézet, nagyítás, szín és tárhely, készlet, ár, mennyiség, kosárba helyezés, specifikációk.

Mobilon egy oszlop és alsó vásárlási sáv. Egyszerre csak egy elsődleges kosárgomb fókuszálható.

CheckoutLayout

S4 · Teljes vásárlás

/penztar/…

Visszafogott fejléc, négylépéses állapotjelző, tartalom és rendelési összesítő. A sikeres demórendelés önálló, visszakereshető állapot.

Kosár → adatok → szállítás/fizetés → ellenőrzés → visszaigazolás. Minden lépéshez saját útvonal, őrfeltétel és hibakezelés tartozik.

Rögzített vizuális szerződés

#FF6A00kiemelés; sötét felirattal
#1B2024fejléc / lábléc
#F5F6F7semleges felület
#A83B00olvasható narancs link

1320 px maximális tartalomszélesség · 24 px fő közök · 48 / 24 / 16 px minimum desktop/tablet/mobil oldalmargó · 8 px kártya- és 6 px vezérlősugár · 44 × 44 px minimum kattintási terület.

A mobil grid 1 oszlop, tableten 2; a szűrő melletti 3 oszlophoz 1280 px felett van elég hely. Ellenőrzési szélességek: 320, 390, 768, 1024, 1440 és 1920 px.

A forrásként kapott raszterben még a korábbi, már nem használt márkanév látható. Az eredeti skill és képei változatlanok maradtak; az új felület élő márkaszövege mindenhol MiBolt. A képen szereplő árakat nem tekintjük adatforrásnak.

Külön hibaképernyő-tervek

Pontos képernyő-specifikáció, útvonalak és hibakezelés

Four screen families, three layout shells

The request is interpreted as homepage + catalogue + product + complete purchase flow. Three reusable shells satisfy the layout requirement without inventing an extra unrelated screen: MarketingLayout, ShopLayout, CheckoutLayout. Cart/contact/delivery/review/success are mandatory states of the fourth family, not omitted screens. This interpretation is fixed in the delivered plan.

Global geometry and visual contract

Use the separately installed mi-bolt-design 1.0.1 unchanged at execution AND verification; it is not embedded in this docs package. The original raster still displays a superseded brand and placeholder commercial claims; preserve it only as source evidence. All live UI is MiBolt. Remove absent-scope services, social/account/newsletter controls and unverified offer promises. This is not a pixel-perfect reconstruction of raster text.

Container max1320px; gutters mobile16px, tablet24px, desktop48px minimum. Desktop 12-column alignment, gap24px, header64px. At1440px content margin60px. 4px spacing grid, mostly8px multiples. Cards8px radius, inputs/buttons6px. Body16px/1.5, support14px, metadata12px only; hero52/34px, H1 36/28px, section28/24px, card18px, price28/26px. Orange#FF6A00 with dark#171B1F button text; dark#1B2024 shell; white shopping panels, subtle#F5F6F7 sections, #A83B00 accessible orange-toned links. Product image ratio4:3 and object-fit contain.

Below640px one catalogue column. 640–1023px two columns with filter drawer. 1024–1279px 264px sidebar plus2-column catalogue. >=1280px sidebar+3 columns. Home featured grid: 1/2/4 by mobile/tablet/desktop. The1280 breakpoint is an explicit content-fit exception to the baseline skill breakpoint set: three cards plus sidebar do not fit comfortably at1024px. It uses the same tokens, not a new visual language.

Every purchase target >=44x44px. Normal controls48px height. Layout must be verified at320,390,768,1024,1440,1920. Motion120–160ms and panel<=220ms, reduced-motion honored. Fixed buy rails reserve their height plus safe-area; no focus can be hidden under a rail.

Route map

Family Route Layout Prerequisite / exit
S1 Home / MarketingLayout catalogue via hero/featured/shortcuts
S2 Catalogue /telefonok + query ShopLayout valid query canonicalization; details route
S3 Product /telefonok/:slug?variant=:variantId ShopLayout product+variant validation; add to cart
S4 Cart /penztar/kosar CheckoutLayout can be empty; next requires valid nonempty cart
S4 Contact /penztar/adatok CheckoutLayout active valid cart
S4 Delivery/payment /penztar/szallitas CheckoutLayout valid saved contact
S4 Review /penztar/ellenorzes CheckoutLayout valid contact/delivery/method + fresh quote
S4 Success /penztar/siker/:orderId CheckoutLayout authoritative receipt lookup, not guard-generated fake data
Utility ** ShopLayout 404 with Home/Catalogue links

/kosar may redirect to canonical /penztar/kosar; do not introduce a second cart state owner. Backend API paths are independent of these UI route names.

S1 — Home

Purpose: give a clear first action and explain the simulation. Header: MiBolt brand left, Mobiltelefonok active nav, A demóról anchor, search and cart right. Compact demo banner immediately below header. Hero charcoal, live heading “A következő mobilod. Egyszerűen.”; accent second line, two concise sentences, primary “Megnézem a telefonokat”, secondary “Hogyan működik?”. Device illustration right; do not put functionality inside an image.

Desktop sequence: 64px header → demo strip → 360–420px hero → three restrained category shortcuts → featured heading/link → four consistent cards → three how-it-works tiles → dark footer. Categories: Összes telefon, 5G készülékek, Raktáron. Featured card CTA “Részletek”. Cards keep unit price and action aligned.

Mobile: readable hero text+CTA before illustration, 16px gutters, stacked shortcut rows, single-column cards, stacked tiles. No compressed desktop screenshot. Search is second header row; menu is accessible drawer. Hero artwork may intentionally crop an illustration but product cards must not crop devices.

States: skeleton featured cards; featured HTTP error+Retry while hero remains usable; image fallback; menu open/focus; CTA hover/focus; cart count changes after navigation. No promotional urgency, ratings, warranty, free shipping or newsletters are invented.

Exact outcome: primary CTA→S2 default. Shortcuts→canonical filters. Featured→exact S3 slug/variant. Explainer is on-page content; no unsupported “plans” route.

S2 — Catalogue

Purpose: find a device and enter a precise variant. Top breadcrumb Főoldal / Mobiltelefonok, H1 Mobiltelefonok, supporting demo sentence. Left264px filters. Main toolbar count, sort and page size. Applied chips between toolbar/grid; 3-column grid at wide desktop, all cards matched in height. Pagination below grid, footer after content.

Filters: q search in header/list toolbar; brands (three fixture brands), storage128/256/512, inclusive whole-HUF min/max,5G,stock. Price range needs two labeled number fields, not a slider requiring a new library. Filter controls expose invalid range and do not send it. 300ms search debounce, immediate desktop filter changes, page reset to1. Sorting: Ajánlott sorrend, Ár szerint növekvő, Ár szerint csökkenő, Név szerint. Page sizes12/24.

Card: 4:3 neutral media surface with small DEMÓTERMÉK tag; live name, max2 features, exact selected storage/color, one-time price with “Egyszeri készülékár • demóadat”, outlined “Részletek →”. Entire card need not be clickable; make image/title/detail link consistent without nested controls.

Mobile filter drawer: header title+named44px close; scrollable fields; footer “Mégse” and “Szűrők alkalmazása”. Copy of applied state remains transient until Apply. Escape/backdrop behaves as cancel; applied URL unchanged. Initial focus=drawer heading, return=filter trigger. Filter counts computed by API, not stale CSS labels.

States: initial loading; current data refreshing with small progress; empty with current filters+Reset;503/network failure with Retry and retained query; invalid price; image error; out-of-range page canonicalization; back/forward restoration. Loading/error/no-results are not the same component message. Complete rows are listed in docs/tasks/test-scenarios.json.

S3 — Product details

Purpose: inspect media/specifications, select exact configuration, add an available quantity. Shared shop shell/breadcrumb. Desktop left7 columns gallery, right5 columns product/configuration; light shopping canvas, no hero. Right: small DEMÓTERMÉK marker, H1 Demo One5G, description, stock, color/storage selectors, unit price, quantity, primary “Kosárba teszem”, simulation note. More specifications below main content in disclosures. No subscription configurator.

Gallery: reserved main stage, previous/next44px targets, live counter, thumbnail row. Zoom opens real modal with named close and contained focus. The demo uses authorized/user-provided artwork as illustrative device media; mock data must never describe it as a verified real phone. Asset manifest links each view/variant to an explicit media source. A back-view image cannot be replaced by a front image while labeled back.

Variant matrix: exact combination is chosen, with color name beside swatch and selected check. Unavailable combinations disabled with reason. A variant change updates image, price, capacity, stock and max quantity atomically. Invalid deep-link variant yields explanation and blocked add until valid selection. Noninteger/out-of-limit quantity rejected, not silently changed.

Mobile: one column gallery then product info/options; specs follow. A compact sticky rail shows price+same add action and status; only one responsive primary control is focusable at a time. Scroll padding, safe-area and keyboard focus are validated. No auto-rotating carousel or baseline3D placeholder button.

States: loading; notfound; request error/Retry; valid selected; unavailable pair; low stock; image failure; zoom modal; pending add; successful add with persistent named item/variant summary; cart mutation409/503. Failure never erases the selected variant or shows zero price.

S4 — Cart and complete purchase

Common shell: compact dark header, MiBolt left, Demó pénztár label, back-to-phones link. Demo strip, four numbered steps Kosár / Adatok / Szállítás / Ellenőrzés. Success replaces further purchase actions. Desktop8/4-style content+summary columns with24px gap; summary width~360px when space permits, aligned to content top. Mobile single column, summary before final action; steps wrap/read without horizontal page scrolling.

S4.1 Cart

H1 “A kosarad”. Each line: product image, name, color/storage, unit price, quantity control, remove action and line total. Subtotal, shipping “A következő lépésben választod ki”, provisional total only if labeled subtotal. CTA “Tovább az adatokhoz”. Empty H1+message+“Telefonokat nézek”. No free shipping promise or discounts. Pending disables only affected controls and contradictory continue. Remove last item returns empty state.

S4.2 Contact

H1 “Kapcsolattartási adatok”. Full name, email, phone; visible synthetic-data reminder. All fields required; max/range constraints match API contract. Native labels and appropriate input types, field messages, error summary on attempted invalid continue. CTA “Tovább a szállításhoz”; back to cart. Save failure leaves all entries. No login, newsletter, marketing consent or password.

S4.3 Delivery and simulated payment

H1 “Átvétel és fizetés”. Radio cards: Házhoz szállítás – demó (1990Ft) or Demó átvételi pont (0Ft, no real physical location). Conditional address for home delivery: country HU, postcode, city, address line1, optional line2. Payment radios “Utánvét – szimuláció” and “Bankkártya – szimuláció”; neither asks for actual card details. CTA “Tovább az ellenőrzéshez”. Defaults must not represent a paid addon. Any selected method is explicitly visible before final review.

S4.4 Final review

H1 “Minden rendben?”. Item/variant/quantity/price list, contact summary with Módosítás, delivery/method with Módosítás. Current Quote drives subtotal+shipping+total. Two unchecked confirmations: “Elfogadom a demó rendelési feltételeit.” and “Tudomásul veszem, hogy ez egy demó, nem valódi vásárlás.” The demo terms dialog explains no payment/email/shipment and reset/persistence behavior; it is not a production contract.

Primary “Demórendelés véglegesítése” disabled until quote complete/current and both acknowledgements true. Pending stays on same view with meaningful text. Failed payment offers Retry/change method; stale quote offers refresh and re-review; stock loss links cart correction. An uncertain response says processing outcome not known, not definitive rejection. Same request/key retry is mandatory.

S4.5 Successful simulated order

H1 “Elkészült a demórendelésed.” Order DEMO-000001, selected items, final amount, simulated delivery/payment and clear no-real-payment/order/email notice. No “Visszaigazolást küldtünk” claim. CTA “Vissza a telefonokhoz”. Receipt remains through same-tab reload, is retrieved by getOrder and cannot generate another order. Unknown orderID shows404; service error has retry. Cart badge resets from authoritative state.

Required exceptional states and visual evidence

All default/hover/focus/selected/disabled/loading/error/success states apply according to component contracts; read-only sections do not need fabricated hover/error states. Visual screenshots cover all main screens/checkout steps at1440 and390 and the6-width reflow matrix. Additional state boards cover filter-empty/error/loading/drawer, product-unavailable/zoom/image error, cart-empty, contact-invalid, review-submitting/declined/expired/price changed and success. The interactive prototype is a design artifact; final Angular screenshots must be re-created during T25, not mistaken for already verified implementation.

MiBolt MVP · projekt-dokumentáció · 31 feladat / 44 komponens / 16 API-művelet
02 / FELÉPÍTÉS ÉS KOMMUNIKÁCIÓ

Kis komponensek.
Egyértelmű állapotgazdák.

A komponens nem beszél közvetlenül a mock-adatbázissal. A frontend ugyanazt a HTTP-határt használja, mint egy későbbi valódi backend mellett.

Oldal / UIsignal input ↓
output esemény ↑
→
Signal facadeállapot + parancs
readonly UI-nézet
→
Generált SDKng-openapi-gen
Angular HttpClient
→
MSW handlerséma + domain
injektált repository

Mi hol él?

Root: AppConfigFacade és CartFacade; minden layout ugyanazt a kosarat látja.

Lista / termék route: CatalogFacade és ProductFacade; a keresés/variáns URL-állapot, a DTO a válaszokból érkezik.

Pénztári route: CheckoutFacade és OrderFacade; adatok, választások, ajánlat és tranzakció külön érvényességgel.

Komponens lokális állapota: nyitott panel, galériakép, mező-fókusz. Nem másolunk ide üzleti igazságot.

Adatáramlási szabály

A jelenítőkomponens input() adatot kap, output() szándékot küld. A szülő egy facade-parancsot hív. A facade egyszer kezeli az SDK-kérés feliratkozását, a sikeres válasz pedig frissíti a signal állapotot.

A származtatott érték computed(). Keresésnél a régi kérés megszakítható; kosármódosítások sorba rendezettek; rendelésindításnál a második párhuzamos kattintás nem indít új tranzakciót.

A rendelés állapotgépe

editing → quoting → ready → paying / submitting → confirmed

Hiba esetén nem minden út vezet ugyanoda. Lejárt ajánlatnál friss ajánlat kell; elutasított fizetésnél újrapróbálás vagy módszerváltás; bizonytalan hálózati kimenetnél ugyanazt az idempotens rendelési szándékot kell visszakeresni.

Részletes architektúra és komponensdokumentáció

Architektúra, mappaszerkezet, route-ok, állapot és szerződések

MiBolt MVP — architecture specification

Status: implementation-ready design, not an implemented or tested Angular application. The exact contract and task acceptance criteria travel with this file.

1. Chosen approach and alternatives

Chosen: one feature-oriented Angular 22 SPA, small signal facades, generated HTTP SDK and one stateful MSW domain implementation. This preserves a future real-backend boundary while remaining backendless. It is easier to trace and test than mixing HTTP behavior into components.

Rejected for this MVP: a full NgRx entity/effects architecture (extra framework and duplicate abstraction for a small catalogue/cart wizard); a component-local fixture app (cannot demonstrate backend-like validation or SDK/contract parity); operator-specific microfrontends or Web Components design-system cloning (unproven need, greater scope). RxJS is retained only where cancellation/sequencing of generated Observable HTTP calls is useful; signals remain the public UI state.

2. Fixed scope

One fictional MiBolt provider and 16 fictional device products. One-time prices only in whole HUF. No subscriptions, monthly plans, installments, insurance, login, user profiles, real checkout provider, email, account verification, admin UI, product comparison, CMS, localization framework, analytics/social, chat or provider switcher. The supplied research describes operators, but does not authorize their assets, data or proprietary software. Optional O01 adds one licensed/local 3D model only after approval.

Four commerce page families: home, product catalogue, product details, purchase flow. The purchase family owns cart, contact, delivery/payment method, final review and confirmed demo receipt; these are routeable states, not additional unrelated visual systems.

3. Version and build policy

Verified planning baseline on 2026-09-14: Angular 22.1.6 is the latest stable in the official release list; 22.2.0-next builds are not stable. Recheck at T00 and pin the highest stable 22.x. Do not install a newer major by using unqualified @latest. [S01]

Angular 22 stabilized Signal Forms. Use @angular/forms/signals rather than treating Angular 21's experimental status as current. Explicitly use standalone components, signal APIs and built-in control flow. [S03–S06]

The published compatibility table currently lists the 22.0 family with Node ^22.22.3 || ^24.15.0 || ^26.0.0, TypeScript >=6.0.0 <6.1.0, and RxJS ^6.5.3 || ^7.4.0. Select Node 24 LTS in that supported range and verify the actual pinned 22.1.x compiler/package peers before installation; do not infer missing minor-version peer metadata from a table row. The toolchain-lock file records the resolved exact patch versions. [S02]

Use official Angular CLI application/unit-test builders, strict TS/strictTemplates, no Angular Material visual theme, SCSS/CSS semantic tokens, Angular CDK accessibility/dialog utilities where needed. ng-openapi-gen baseline 1.0.5 supports OpenAPI 3.1 and generated standalone API consumption. Explicit promises:false retains cancellation-friendly Observable calls. Do not confuse it with a different package called ng-openapi. [S07]

4. Dependency direction

User / browser navigation
  ↓ signal input / DOM event
Page container (route-scoped composition)
  ↓ explicit facade method
Feature facade (private writable signals, readonly computed view model)
  ↓ generated Api.invoke(operation, typed params)
Angular HttpClient + API-root/session interceptors
  ↓ same-origin /api/v1 HTTP boundary
MSW handler → schema validation → mock domain → repository
  ↑ typed DTO / Problem
Facade → immutable signal update → page → presentational child inputs

No component imports mocks, fixtures, repository, API HttpClient directly or another feature's mutable signals. The only exception to ordinary one-way input/output composition is Signal Forms' field binding, where the form model owner is explicit. Shared presentational UI cannot inject a business facade.

5. State owners and lifetimes

Owner Lifetime Source of truth Exposed reads Commands
AppConfigFacade root getConfig response config/status load, retry
CatalogFacade catalogue route canonical Router query + listProducts response query, vm, resultCount setSearch, setFilters, setSort, setPage, retry
ProductFacade product route getProduct + exact variant query selectedVariant, gallery, vm selectVariant, selectImage, setQuantity, retry
CartFacade root current mock Cart DTO cart, itemCount, subtotal, pending, error ensureCart, addItem, setQuantity, removeItem, refresh
CheckoutFacade /penztar parent volatile Signal Form model + checkout API draft + Quote currentStep, canContinue, quote, submitState saveContact, saveDelivery, refreshQuote, submit, retryUnknownOutcome
Dialog/Menu local component/service opened flag/trigger reference opened open, close
MockRepository each demo tab/test context domain catalogue/cart/draft/intents/orders/idempotency maps handlers only atomic mutation, reset, snapshot

Derived totals displayed in purchase screens come from the returned Cart/Quote/OrderReceipt; UI may compute quantity sum for presentation only if tested against authoritative DTO. Do not maintain two independently writable totals. DTOs are readonly inputs to views; facades own immutable replacements.

6. Communication contracts

CatalogQuery contains q, unique brands, unique storageGb, optional minPrice/maxPrice, inStock/is5g, sort, page and pageSize. Router query names on the wire are singular brand and repeated storageGb. URI codec sorts repeated values, trims q, removes empty/default filters and uses page=1 after any filter/sort change. Unknown browser query values are cleaned with explanation where meaningful; direct invalid HTTP query returns 400. Page out of range gets the final valid page once; zero total stays page=1.

AsyncState<T> is a tagged union: {status:'idle'|'loading'}, {status:'success',data:T,refreshing:boolean}, {status:'empty',data:T}, {status:'error',problem:UiProblem,previous?:T}. A network error is never recoded as []. Error state may preserve the previous rendered list with an explicit warning and disabled unsafe actions.

CatalogFacade.setFilters(next: CatalogFilters): void changes Router query, not an unrelated BehaviorSubject. ProductFacade.selectVariant(id: string): void updates the exact variant query and resets invalid gallery index. CartFacade.addItem(command: {variantId:string;quantity:number}): void enqueues a serialized write and owns its single managed subscription. A private Observable pipeline performs the SDK request; page event handlers invoke the void command, never subscribe to a second cold request. CheckoutFacade.submit(): void exhausts a concurrent second click and advances the state machine only on authoritative responses.

Read requests use switchMap to cancel obsolete HTTP and prevent late results. Cart writes are serialized with concatMap and take expected version when dispatched. Order submission uses exhaust semantics: never switchMap a transactional write. Every subscription is bounded by completion or takeUntilDestroyed. Effects are limited to genuine side effects such as title/focus/persistence; computed values are not propagated with cascading effects. [S05,S06]

7. Suggested source structure

# mi-bolt-design is resolved externally; no duplicate skill folder in docs
docs/api/openapi.yaml                single wire contract
docs/api/fixtures/                   deterministic synthetic data
src/main.ts                     bootstrap entry, fatal fallback
src/core/bootstrap-demo.ts      await mock hydrate/start, then Angular
src/styles/tokens.css           copied/generated semantic tokens
src/app/app.ts                  root outlet, global announcements
src/app/app.config.ts           locale/router/http/config providers
src/app/app.routes.ts           lazy page/layout boundaries
src/app/api/generated/          ng-openapi-gen output, no manual edits
src/app/core/api/               API root and demo-session interceptor
src/app/core/navigation/        title and route-focus behavior
src/app/layouts/                MarketingLayout, ShopLayout, CheckoutLayout
src/app/shared/ui/              primitives only, no business injection
src/app/shared/shell/           Header, Footer, DemoNotice, Menu
src/app/features/home/          hero, featured products, explainer
src/app/features/catalog/       URL codec, facade, filters and catalogue
src/app/features/product/       facade, variant matrix, gallery, specs
src/app/features/cart/          root facade, serialized queue, lines/summary
src/app/features/checkout/      parent facade, forms, guards, steps, submit
src/mocks/                     shared handlers/domain/repository/scenarios
public/mockServiceWorker.js    generated by MSW tooling
public/assets/products/        authorized local images only
public/assets/models/          absent until O01 approved
 tests/                         Node/Angular unit and SDK-contract tests
 scripts/                       generation, parity, policy and release gates

Tests live beside feature units where useful; top-level tests owns shared/contract fixtures. Do not create a monorepo or split repository unless there is a real second application. All production runtime code remains feature-local and participates in the explicit coverage manifest.

8. MSW repository and persistence

createHandlers({repository,clock,ids,scenario,apiRoot}) returns the same handlers for browser setupWorker and Node setupServer. Handlers are thin: parse/validate, call domain, format response. MSW browser handlers run on the page side; the MSW service worker is an interception bridge, not a persistent backend database. Never assume its memory survives page reload. Await start before bootstrap to avoid startup races. [S08–S10]

Use a minimal dependency-free sessionStorage snapshot adapter for tab-scoped demo persistence, behind a typed interface. Memory implementation serves tests and storage-denied mode. Snapshot allowlist: schemaVersion, providerId, simulatedSessionId, savedAt/expiresAt, cart identity/items/version, simulated inventory, privacy-minimized receipts, safe order intent references/key digests and terminal order replay result. Never serialize contact, address, full checkout draft, raw request bodies or arbitrary registry maps.

Cart TTL and committed-order idempotency retention: 24 mock-clock hours. Quote TTL: 5 minutes. Any stale/schema-invalid persisted snapshot is discarded with a user-visible recovery. Before ordinary full reload, contact/address remain volatile by design: the cart restores, earliest incomplete checkout step reopens and explains the reset. Within one SPA session an HTTP/validation failure preserves all typed fields. Storage denied still supports a complete in-memory demo but explicitly warns that reload cannot restore it.

X-Demo-Session is an opaque namespace, NOT security. Distinct browser contexts have separate sessionStorage and repositories. A duplicated browser tab may copy sessionStorage; each page still uses isolated mock memory. True multi-tab concurrent commerce, authentication and server trust are outside scope; do not market session IDs as an authorization scheme.

9. Cart, quote, payment, order invariants

All prices originate from fixture catalogue values, not incoming requests. Money.amount is whole HUF, not ISO minor units. Avoid decimal multiplication. Item quantity 1..5 and <= available stock; reject rather than clamp. Same variant merges into one cart line. A successful cart mutation increments its version once and invalidates quote and intent. Draft updates invalidate quote/intent without changing cart version; UI serializes draft writes.

For mutations use If-Match: "<cart.version>" and Idempotency-Key. Idempotency lookup happens before comparing current cart status/version: a replay after success must not become a conflict. Scope key to session+method+path and canonical request fingerprint, including semantically relevant version precondition. A retry sends the identical body/precondition. Reusing a key with a different payload returns 409. Store terminal success or domain-conflict outcome for replay; transport errors before execution have no commit; failed schema validation mutates nothing. A PRICE_CHANGED quote response may atomically update cart/version and stores that conflict outcome so a retry cannot update it again.

Quotes do NOT reserve stock. Commit rechecks current inventory atomically. Price changes refresh authoritative cart and require explicit review. Quote cannot be used after an earlier step changes or 5 minutes pass. Card simulation accepts no card fields: createPaymentIntent is a same-origin MSW request, with deterministic succeeded/declined/pending status. Pending GET polling: 1s interval, maximum 10 attempts; surface unknown outcome on timeout.

For order commit: revalidate quote/session/cart/stock, require current demo terms+simulation acknowledgement, require matching succeeded intent only for demo_card, persist receipt and idempotency result, mark cart ordered, decrement stock exactly once, then clear volatile PII. Full rollback on conflict; never a partially stored receipt. A timeout-after-commit retries the same key/body. Successful replay uses persisted receipt before examining cleared volatile draft. Recovery without an actual commit returns to data entry because personal fields were intentionally not persisted.

10. Checkout transition model

stateDiagram-v2
  [*] --> Cart
  Cart --> Contact: nonempty valid cart
  Contact --> Delivery: validated contact saved
  Delivery --> Review: valid delivery/method saved + fresh quote
  Review --> Contact: edit contact
  Review --> Delivery: edit delivery
  Review --> Review: refreshed quote / correction
  Review --> Payment: valid consent + demo_card
  Payment --> Review: declined / unresolved / recoverable error
  Payment --> Commit: matching intent succeeded
  Review --> Commit: valid consent + demo_cod
  Commit --> Review: definite pre-commit error
  Commit --> Recover: uncertain network outcome
  Recover --> Commit: exact same key and body
  Commit --> Success: authoritative receipt
  Success --> Cart: new shopping intent

No route guard performs mutation. Direct invalid step access goes to earliest valid prerequisite. Success route always reads getOrder and never repeats POST. Browser Back after success cannot resubmit an ordered cart.

11. Static deployment and maintenance

Same-origin logical API defaults /api/v1 but runtime root is resolved under the deployment base. Both SDK and MSW use the same resolved base. Media paths from contract are logical /assets paths and resolve under base; never naively prepend the current route. Worker script scope must contain all app routes and API calls. HTTPS/localhost is required for browser MSW, unlike the delivered offline design prototype.

Serve SPA fallback for app routes only; /api/v1 fails without MSW rather than returning HTML. index and mock worker use no-cache; hashed chunks use immutable caching. Do not register Angular PWA/service-worker caching. Unregister only the known obsolete MiBolt worker at its known scope when needed; never clear unrelated origin workers. Business actions are blocked on mock-start failure, not routed to a real service.

Budget targets (project decisions, not current measurements): initial JS 300 KiB gzip, lazy route 150 KiB gzip, initial-view transfer 1 MiB excluding optional 3D, CLS <=0.1. Test at a recorded desktop and narrow emulated profile. Accessibility and resource audits require an optimized build separate from an instrumentation build.

18. Supplied local device art and asset URL boundary

The plan supplies original fictional front/back artwork in docs/design/assets/products, with docs/design/asset-manifest.json provenance. Copy the WebP files to public/assets/products/ in T10. These files do not claim real-device fidelity or contain functional UI text. All fixture media references resolve to an included image; matching casing color and view are explicit.

ProductImage uses resolveAssetUrl(path: string, documentBaseUri: string): string from src/app/shared/media/resolve-asset-url.ts. Only a normalized logical /assets/ path with no .., encoded traversal, protocol-relative or absolute-URL input is accepted. Strip the leading slash and resolve against document.baseURI; reject any resulting URL outside the configured same-origin base assets prefix. Test the / deployment and malformed paths. No untrusted remote image fetch is allowed.

Minden komponens részletes szerződése és kommunikációs minták

Component ownership and communication contracts

Every component execution and verification MUST use mi-bolt-design. Public inputs use input()/input.required(); intents use output(). Private local changes use signal(); derived view-models use computed(). Signal Forms fields are the deliberate parent-owned two-way binding exception. No presentation component knows the SDK or repository.

Shared types

CatalogFilters = {brands: readonly string[]; storageGb: readonly (128|256|512)[]; minPrice?:number; maxPrice?:number; inStock:boolean; is5g:boolean}. CatalogQuery = CatalogFilters & {q:string; sort:Sort; page:number; pageSize:12|24}. Sort = recommended | price_asc | price_desc | name_asc. Facets is the ProductPage.facets schema. FilterIdentity = {name: keyof CatalogFilters; value?:string|number}. CheckoutStep = cart | contact | delivery | review | success. AsyncStatus = idle | loading | success | empty | error. UiProblem = {code:string; message:string; fieldErrors:FieldError[]; retryable:boolean}. CheckoutDraft composes optional Contact, DeliverySelection and paymentMethod. ReviewState = quoting | ready | expired | price-changed | stock-error | submitting | error. PaymentStatus = idle | pending | succeeded | declined | unknown | error. CategoryShortcut = {label:string; query:Partial<CatalogQuery>}. LicensedLocalModel = {src:string; licenseReference:string; variantId:string}. ViewerError = load | decode | unsupported. FieldTree<T> is Angular Signal Forms typed field-tree API, not a handwritten replacement. Wire types Money,Cart,Variant,Quote,Image,FieldError are generated from OpenAPI.

Example: precise signal input/output boundary

This is an implementation pattern, not a compiled or tested deliverable in this planning package.

import { ChangeDetectionStrategy, Component, computed, input, output } from '@angular/core';
import type { Variant } from '../../api/generated/models/variant';

@Component({
  selector: 'mi-add-to-cart',
  changeDetection: ChangeDetectionStrategy.OnPush,
  template: `
    <button type="button" [disabled]="blocked()" (click)="requestAdd()">
      @if (pending()) { Hozzáadás… } @else { Kosárba teszem }
    </button>
    @if (variant()?.availability === 'out_of_stock') {
      <p role="status">Ez a változat most nem rendelhető a demóban.</p>
    }
  `,
})
export class AddToCartControl {
  readonly variant = input<Variant | null>(null);
  readonly quantity = input.required<number>();
  readonly pending = input(false);
  readonly addRequested = output<{variantId: string; quantity: number}>();
  readonly blocked = computed(() => {
    const v = this.variant();
    const n = this.quantity();
    return this.pending() || !v || v.availability !== 'in_stock'
      || !Number.isInteger(n) || n < 1 || n > Math.min(v.maxQuantity, 5);
  });
  requestAdd(): void {
    const v = this.variant();
    if (!v || this.blocked()) return;
    this.addRequested.emit({variantId: v.id, quantity: this.quantity()});
  }
}

Parent: [variant]="product.vm().selectedVariant" [quantity]="product.vm().quantity" [pending]="cart.pending()" (addRequested)="cart.addItem($event)" is a conceptual binding. The public CartFacade.addItem(command): void command owns subscription/queue lifecycle. It invokes a private Observable queue subscribed exactly once; no container subscription and no un-subscribed cold Observable are allowed. The readonly DTO is never mutated by the child.

Communication examples

  1. Filter change: FilterPanel output → CatalogPage → CatalogFacade Router write → generated listProducts Observable → MSW → immutable vm signal → toolbar/cards/chips. URL is the shared state, not events between sibling cards.
  2. Variant add: VariantSelector output → ProductFacade exact selection → ProductGallery/PriceBlock inputs → AddToCartControl intent → CartFacade mutation queue → SDK/MSW → Cart signal → header/cart/summary. No unrelated component writes the badge.
  3. Submit: ReviewStep output → CheckoutFacade verifies Signal Form/consent/current Quote → order orchestrator → optional mock intent → createOrder → returned receipt → Router success → SuccessStep getOrder. A UI click alone cannot produce a success state.
MiBolt MVP · projekt-dokumentáció · 31 feladat / 44 komponens / 16 API-művelet
03 / CONTRACT-FIRST API ÉS MOCK

Mock backend nélkül.
Nem backendlogika nélkül.

OpenAPI 3.1.0, 32 séma és 16 művelet. A mock ugyanúgy validál, hibázik, számol és állapotot módosít, mint az elvárt valódi szolgáltatás.

OpenAPI YAMLSDK-generátor konfiguráció
A szerződés az adatmodell közös forrása; nem automatikus üzleti logika.

A kérés/válasz sémák és a generált típusok innen származnak. A készletkezelés, ajánlatlejárat, tranzakció és idempotencia külön, ugyanerre a szerződésre épülő MSW domainmegvalósítás. Az SDK-kimenetet tilos kézzel módosítani. [S07]

MűveletÚtvonaloperationIdVálasz
GET/configgetConfig200 · DemoConfig
GET/productslistProducts200 · ProductPage
GET/products/{slug}getProduct200 · Product
POST/cartscreateCart201 · Cart
GET/carts/{cartId}getCart200 · Cart
POST/carts/{cartId}/itemsaddCartItem200 · Cart
PATCH/carts/{cartId}/items/{itemId}setCartItemQuantity200 · Cart
DELETE/carts/{cartId}/items/{itemId}removeCartItem200 · Cart
GET/checkout/optionsgetCheckoutOptions200 · CheckoutOptions
GET/checkout/{cartId}getCheckoutSession200 · CheckoutSession
PUT/checkout/{cartId}updateCheckout200 · CheckoutSession
POST/checkout/{cartId}/quotecreateCheckoutQuote200 · Quote
POST/payment-intentscreatePaymentIntent201 · PaymentIntent
GET/payment-intents/{paymentIntentId}getPaymentIntent200 · PaymentIntent
POST/orderscreateOrder201 · OrderReceipt
GET/orders/{orderId}getOrder200 · OrderReceipt

Logikai API-gyökér: /api/v1. Ugyanazon origin, a telepítési baseHref-hez igazítva. A felületi magyar útvonalak ettől függetlenek.

Állapot és konzisztencia

If-Match a kosárverzióhoz, Idempotency-Key az írásokhoz. Azonos kulcs és azonos törzs ugyanazt az eredményt adja. Eltérő törzs ugyanazzal a kulccsal 409.

Az idempotens visszajátszás megelőzi a már módosult kosár / már lezárt tranzakció ellenőrzését. Így a válasz elvesztése nem eredményez dupla rendelést.

Készletcsökkentés, rendeléslétrehozás és kosárlezárás egy atomi mock-művelet.

Számok és érvényesség

Money.amount egész forint, nem a pénznem ISO legkisebb egysége. Nincs lebegőpontos pénzszámítás.

339 990 × 2 = 679 980 Ft.
Házhoz szállítással = 681 970 Ft.

Mennyiség 1…min(készlet, 5); ajánlat 5 percig érvényes. A szerveroldali szerepet betöltő mock számítja a végösszeget.

Frissítés és személyes adatok

A kosár, a készlet és a személyes adat nélküli demóvisszaigazolás saját böngészőfülön belül visszaállítható, engedélyezett sessionStorage snapshotból. A handler kód nem egy tartós service-worker-adatbázisban él; a perzisztenciát külön adapter kezeli.

A név, e-mail, telefonszám, cím, nyers kéréstörzs és függő fizetési adat nem kerül böngészőtárhelyre. Teljes újratöltésnél a befejezetlen pénztár visszalép az adatokhoz; a kosár megmarad, a felület ezt érthetően jelzi. SPA-visszalépés és hálózati hiba nem törli a kitöltést.

Tárhelyhiba esetén dokumentált memóriamód és figyelmeztetés. Az X-Demo-Session csak izolációs névtér, nem hitelesítés.

A mock indulási sorrendje

snapshot ellenőrzés → repository → worker.start() → Angular bootstrap

Ugyanaz a handlerkészlet fut a böngészőben setupWorker és a unit tesztekben setupServer alatt. Ismeretlen üzleti kérés hiba; élő API-ra visszaesés nincs. [S08–S10]

A teljes OpenAPI-leíró megtekintése
openapi: 3.1.0
info:
  title: MiBolt Demo Commerce API
  version: 1.0.0
  description: Contract-first Angular MVP. Every operation is implemented with MSW;
    no live backend, billing, email or fulfillment. Demo values are fictional. JSON
    Schema constraints plus documented domain invariants are mandatory. All validation
    text is hu-HU at the UI boundary.
servers:
- url: /api/v1
  description: Same-origin logical endpoint; served only by MSW. Runtime root respects
    the Angular base href.
tags:
- name: Configuration
- name: Catalog
- name: Cart
- name: Checkout
- name: Payment
- name: Orders
security: []
paths:
  /config:
    get:
      operationId: getConfig
      tags:
      - Configuration
      summary: Read deterministic MiBolt demo configuration
      description: Read deterministic MiBolt demo configuration
      parameters: []
      responses:
        '200':
          description: Successful response
          content:
            application/json:
              schema:
                $ref: '#/components/schemas/DemoConfig'
        '503':
          description: Typed application or simulated transport failure; see Problem.code.
          content:
            application/problem+json:
              schema:
                $ref: '#/components/schemas/Problem'
  /products:
    get:
      operationId: listProducts
      tags:
      - Catalog
      summary: Search and filter product catalogue
      description: 'Filters apply conjunctively to one SAME variant. Returned defaultVariant
        is the cheapest matching variant with ID tie-break. Facet counts are disjunctive:
        remove own facet and count distinct matching products under all others. total
        counts products, never variants. Unknown query parameters return 400. An out-of-range
        page returns empty items with unchanged total; UI canonicalizes to final valid
        page with one refetch.'
      parameters:
      - name: q
        in: query
        schema:
          type: string
          maxLength: 100
        description: Case-insensitive NFC-normalized substring over brand/name; trimmed.
        style: form
        explode: true
      - name: brand
        in: query
        schema:
          type: array
          items:
            type: string
        description: Repeat brand=A&brand=B; OR within this facet, AND across facets.
        style: form
        explode: true
      - name: storageGb
        in: query
        schema:
          type: array
          items:
            type: integer
            enum:
            - 128
            - 256
            - 512
        description: Repeat parameter; OR within facet.
        style: form
        explode: true
      - name: minPrice
        in: query
        schema:
          type: integer
          minimum: 0
        description: Whole HUF inclusive; evaluated against variants, not unrelated
          cheapest variant.
        style: form
        explode: true
      - name: maxPrice
        in: query
        schema:
          type: integer
          minimum: 0
        description: Must be >= minPrice.
        style: form
        explode: true
      - name: inStock
        in: query
        schema:
          type: boolean
        description: ''
        style: form
        explode: true
      - name: is5g
        in: query
        schema:
          type: boolean
        description: ''
        style: form
        explode: true
      - name: featured
        in: query
        schema:
          type: boolean
        description: ''
        style: form
        explode: true
      - name: sort
        in: query
        schema:
          type: string
          enum:
          - recommended
          - price_asc
          - price_desc
          - name_asc
          default: recommended
        description: Stable product ID tie-break. Matching selected variant determines
          price sorting.
        style: form
        explode: true
      - name: page
        in: query
        schema:
          type: integer

components:
  schemas:
    Money:
      type: object
      additionalProperties: false
      required:
      - amount
      - currency
      properties:
        amount:
          type: integer
          minimum: 0
          maximum: 100000000
          description: Whole Hungarian forints, NOT ISO minor units; no floating point
            or monthly fees.
        currency:
          type: string
          enum:
          - HUF
    Image:
      type: object
      additionalProperties: false
      required:
      - id
      - src
      - alt
      - width
      - height
      properties:
        id:
          type: string
        src:
          type: string
          pattern: ^/assets/
        alt:
          type: string
          minLength: 1
        width:
          type: integer
          minimum: 1
        height:
          type: integer
          minimum: 1
    Availability:
      type: string
      enum:
      - in_stock
      - out_of_stock
    Variant:
      type: object
      additionalProperties: false
      required:
      - id
      - colorCode
      - colorName
      - swatchHex
      - storageGb
      - price
      - availability
      - maxQuantity
      - images
      properties:
        id:
          type: string
        colorCode:
          type: string
        colorName:
          type: string
        swatchHex:
          type: string
          pattern: ^#[0-9A-Fa-f]{6}$
        storageGb:
          type: integer
          enum:
          - 128
          - 256
          - 512

          $ref: '#/components/schemas/Availability'

          type: integer
          minimum: 0
          maximum: 5
        images:

          items:
            $ref: '#/components/schemas/Image'

04 / DEKOMPOZÍCIÓ ÉS FÜGGŐSÉGEK

30 alapfeladat.
+1 opcionális 3D.

Minden csomag saját kimenetet, konkrét fájlfelelősséget, be-/kimeneti szerződést, tesztlépéseket és elfogadási kritériumokat tartalmaz. A gráf függőségi sorrend, nem becsült naptári ütemezés.

A coverage-megoldás bizonyítása korai blokkoló kapu: T02.

Még a funkciófejlesztés előtt igazolni kell a hiányzó fájl, kimaradt ág, lazy kód és teljes újratöltés mérését. Nem a projekt végén derülhet ki, hogy a „100%” riport valójában hiányos.

Dependency gráf

A nyíl előfeltételből a rá épülő feladatra mutat. A csomópontra kattintva a feladatlap nyílik meg. O01 csak jóváhagyás után része a release-kapunak; ekkor T29 függőségeihez is hozzáadandó.

Teljes vásárlásT25–T29

Szigorú elfogadási kritériumok

T00-AC01Check npm metadata and official release notes; choose maximum non-prerelease Angular major 22, not latest across majors. Record exact Angular/CLI/build/CDK, Node, TypeScript, RxJS, MSW, Vitest and ng-openapi-gen versions.
T00-AC02Use Angular 22.1.6 as the verified 2026-09-14 baseline, re-resolving newer stable 22.x at execution. Verify actual pinned peer dependencies and reject an unsupported Node/TypeScript version.
T00-AC03Resolve and validate the separately installed mi-bolt-design skill (approved version 1.0.1); record its actual location, SHA-256 and version in docs/mi-bolt/execution/skill-location.json. Do not copy or reinstall it from this documentation package. Missing skill or missing implementation/review evidence must fail the gate.
T00-AC04AGENTS.md explicitly makes mi-bolt-design mandatory for EVERY implementation and verification, including nonvisual tasks with reasoned not-applicable R-rules.
T00-AC05Record four screen families, three layout shells, fictional products, one provider, one-time HUF prices and optional 3D as the only open product decision.

A feladatlap megnyitása ↗

Teljes végrehajtási és ellenőrzési feladatlap

T00 — Követelmények, verziózár és kötelező design-governance

Status: PLANNED — this packet is an implementation specification, not execution evidence.

Mandatory entry and review gate

The mi-bolt-design skill is strictly mandatory for every execution and every verification of this task. Read the externally installed mi-bolt-design skill entrypoint recorded in docs/mi-bolt/execution/skill-location.json (approved version 1.0.1) and its required references independently at both stages. The skill is managed separately and is deliberately not included in this documentation package. Do not skip the skill for API, test or build tasks: record applicable rules and explicit nonvisual N/A decisions.

Dependency and ownership

Depends on: none. Area: Foundation. Topological wave: 0 (dependency depth, not a promised time schedule). Optional: false.

Deliverable: Reproducible environment and enforceable design-review policy.

Files

  • AGENTS.md
  • docs/mi-bolt/execution/skill-location.json
  • docs/mi-bolt/execution/decisions.md
  • docs/mi-bolt/execution/toolchain-lock.json

Contract with neighboring tasks

Input: delivered specification and mi-bolt-design 1.0.1. Output: immutable project constraints, stable Angular-22 lock manifest and review evidence schema.

The full wire contract is docs/api/openapi.yaml; UI/domain interfaces are in docs/spec/components.md and docs/spec/architecture.md. Never introduce an incompatible signature as an implementation detail.

Szigorú elfogadási kritériumok

Szigorú elfogadási kritériumok

T05-AC01Await persistence hydration and worker.start() before bootstrapApplication; render explicit retryable fatal state if registration/startup fails.
T05-AC02Use the same handler factory in setupWorker and setupServer. Clock, IDs, delays and repository are injected, never unseeded randomness.
T05-AC03Persist only allowlisted non-PII snapshot fields in tab-scoped sessionStorage; clear malformed/expired/version-mismatched snapshots safely. Storage-denied mode remains functional in memory and announces no reload persistence.
T05-AC04Contact, address, draft, pending intent and raw idempotency payloads are not serialized. Persist privacy-minimized committed order receipts and order-key digests for uncertain-response recovery.
T05-AC05Unknown API endpoints cannot pass through; local app/media assets are explicitly allowed. External scripts, fonts, telemetry and business hosts are disallowed.
T05-AC06E2E: no end-to-end suite by the product owner's decision of 2026-09-20; rendered surfaces are verified by the real-browser pass in docs/spec/testing.md

A feladatlap megnyitása ↗

  • [ ] T05-G03 For UI/state changes use Angular signals, signal inputs/outputs and built-in template control flow. Consume API data exclusively through the generated SDK and MSW boundary.
  • [ ] T05-G04 All applicable unit acceptance scenarios are automated against deterministic mocks. New shared/browser runtime files must appear in both coverage manifests, including unexecuted files. Node-only targets must appear in their separate 100% unit and black-box command/integration coverage manifests; no silent exclusions.
  • [ ] T05-G05 No live business API, telemetry, remote font, payment, email or SaaS request; no test skip/focus, coverage-ignore comment, threshold reduction or hidden generated-file editing.

T07Állapottartó kosár mockT05
MockTERVEZETT — nincs készre jelentve

Átadandó eredmény: Authoritative simulated cart, not component-local fixture arrays.

Fájlfelelősség

src/mocks/cart/cart.handlers.tssrc/mocks/cart/cart-domain.tssrc/mocks/idempotency.tstests/cart-mock.spec.ts

Szigorú elfogadási kritériumok

T07-AC01Repeated variant merges; different variant remains separate. Add and absolute update reject quantity outside 1..min(stock,5); never silently clamp.
T07-AC02Only successful atomic cart mutations increment version once. A quote/payment is invalidated on mutation; totals are whole HUF from catalogue prices.
T07-AC03Same method/path/session/key and normalized payload replay before version checks; different payload returns IDEMPOTENCY_CONFLICT.
T07-AC04409 conflicts preserve visible cart until authoritative refetch; 410 expiry creates an explicit recovery choice, not silent checkout continuation.
T07-AC05No deletion/update succeeds on another session cart; session scoping is isolation, not production-grade authentication.

T08Checkout draft, szállítás és árajánlat mockT07

Szigorú elfogadási kritériumok

T08-AC03Quote requires nonempty cart, complete contact/delivery/payment and current stock; total = subtotal + shipping.
T08-AC04Quote expires after exactly 5 mock-clock minutes; stale cart version or draft updates invalidate it.
T08-AC05Catalogue price changes update cart/version atomically and return PRICE_CHANGED; UI must re-fetch and obtain explicit review of the new amount before order.

T09Szimulált fizetés és idempotens rendelés mockT08
MockTERVEZETT — nincs készre jelentve

Fájlfelelősség

src/mocks/payment/payment.handlers.tssrc/mocks/orders/order.handlers.tssrc/mocks/orders/commit-order.tstests/order-mock.spec.ts

Szigorú elfogadási kritériumok

T09-AC01Only demo_card and demo_cod exist. No bank-card fields or third-party payment requests are accepted.

T09-AC03Atomic order commit checks consent/version/stock/expiry, decrements simulated stock once, stores receipt plus idempotency replay and marks cart ordered.
T09-AC04A timeout-after-commit followed by exact key/body retry returns the same order without decrementing stock twice; different body with reused key returns 409.
T09-AC05Reload can recover the same privacy-minimized receipt. Contact/address are absent from persisted receipt, logs and browser screenshot evidence.
T09-AC06Success copy explicitly says no real payment, order transmission, email or delivery occurred.

  • [ ] T09-G04 All applicable unit acceptance scenarios are automated against deterministic mocks. New shared/browser runtime files must appear in both coverage manifests, including unexecuted files. Node-only targets must appear in their separate 100% unit and black-box command/integration coverage manifests; no silent exclusions.
  • [ ] T09-G05 No live business API, telemetry, remote font, payment, email or SaaS request; no test skip/focus, coverage-ignore comment, threshold reduction or hidden generated-file editing.

T12Katalógus URL-state és signal facadeT04 · T06 · T11
StateTERVEZETT — nincs készre jelentve

Átadandó eredmény: A single URL source of truth with cancellation-safe data reads.

Komponens-/API-szerződés: CatalogQuery codec; CatalogFacade.vm: Signal<CatalogVm>; setFilters, setSort, setPage, setSearch, retry.

Fájlfelelősség

src/app/features/catalog/catalog-query.tssrc/app/features/catalog/catalog.facade.tssrc/app/features/catalog/catalog.facade.spec.ts

Szigorú elfogadási kritériumok

T12-AC01Normalize empty/default/unknown query values with the documented canonical URL codec; round-trip brand arrays and storage arrays without duplicates.
T12-AC02Search debounces 300 ms; filter/sort changes reset page to 1. Use switchMap or equivalent cancellation for generated Observable reads.
T12-AC03A deliberately slow old response never overwrites a newer query; no effect loop causes duplicate requests.
T12-AC04Distinct loading/refreshing/success/empty/error states preserve existing useful content; errors never turn into zero results.
T12-AC05Browser back/forward and direct links restore search, applied filters, page and sort; mobile draft filters remain local until Apply.

A feladatlap megnyitása ↗

T15Termék- és változatkezelő facadeT04 · T06

T15-AC05Quantity retains only a valid explicit selection; display validation rather than hidden clamping when variant stock becomes insufficient.

T17Globális kosár signal facadeT04 · T07
StateTERVEZETT — nincs készre jelentve

Fájlfelelősség

src/app/features/cart/cart.facade.tssrc/app/features/cart/cart-mutation-queue.tssrc/app/features/cart/cart.facade.spec.ts

Szigorú elfogadási kritériumok

T17-AC01Header, product add and checkout read the same root-scoped CartFacade; no sibling event bus or duplicate totals.
T17-AC02Serialize mutations and bind expected version to each actual dispatch, not enqueue time. Use new idempotency key for a new intent, same key only for retry.
T17-AC03Wait for authoritative mutation response before changing line totals; pending controls prevent contradictory writes.
T17-AC04409 requires re-fetch then explicit user correction; do not replay an absolute quantity against newer state without consent.
T17-AC05401 is not invented for this unauthenticated demo; documented 404/410/storage-loss paths yield explicit recovery.

T19Checkout scope, Signal Forms és route guardokT08 · T18
StateTERVEZETT — nincs készre jelentve

Átadandó eredmény: Stateful wizard with explicit recovery and validation.

Komponens-/API-szerződés: CheckoutFacade scoped to checkout parent; state machine cart→contact→delivery→review→submitting→success with error return paths.

Fájlfelelősség

src/app/features/checkout/checkout.facade.tssrc/app/features/checkout/checkout.routes.tssrc/app/features/checkout/checkout.guards.tssrc/app/features/checkout/checkout-form.model.ts

Szigorú elfogadási kritériumok

T19-AC01Use stable Angular 22 Signal Forms from @angular/forms/signals; signals own form data and validity; no duplicate FormGroup/BehaviorSubject source.
T19-AC02Direct deep link or full reload of incomplete checkout redirects to earliest incomplete step with explanation; cart survives but contact/address are cleared on reload.
T19-AC03Back navigation preserves in-memory fields; a declined payment/order error does not reset contact, address, method or cart.
T19-AC04Each page can advance only after its own inputs validate and draft save succeeds. Editing earlier data invalidates quote/intent.
T19-AC05No guard submits an order or starts payment; all writes require explicit UI actions.

CheckoutFacade scoped to checkout parent; state machine cart→contact→delivery→review→submitting→success with error return paths.

Independent test/review cycle

  • [ ] Read this packet, dependency outputs, docs/spec/acceptance.md, docs/spec/testing.md, and mi-bolt-design with relevant references.
  • [ ] Add a named failing test for EACH criterion below to the owned spec files, before its implementation. UI criteria require rendered DOM/user-action assertions; API criteria require actual SDK requests intercepted by MSW.
  • [ ] Execute the focused tests and capture a failure caused by the missing behavior, not an import/configuration error. Use the exact scenarios for test groups: checkout, guards in docs/tasks/test-scenarios.json.
  • [ ] Implement one criterion at a time in the listed ownership boundary. Keep transport, state, presentation and mock domain logic separate.
  • [ ] Run the focused tests, then the project gates documented in docs/spec/testing.md; add new files to both source-coverage inventories, preserving the 100% thresholds.
  • [ ] Independently reload mi-bolt-design and verify relevant rendered states at the stated viewports. Attach evidence for all applicable R01–R26 rules, not a self-reported 'looks good'.
  • [ ] Record actual commands, failures/fixes and final outputs in docs/mi-bolt/execution/tasks/T19.md; commit a single independently reviewable deliverable after acceptance.

Strict task-specific acceptance criteria

  • [ ] T19-AC01 Use stable Angular 22 Signal Forms from @angular/forms/signals; signals own form data and validity; no duplicate FormGroup/BehaviorSubject source.
  • [ ] T19-AC02 Direct deep link or full reload of incomplete checkout redirects to earliest incomplete step with explanation; cart survives but contact/address are cleared on reload.
  • [ ] T19-AC03 Back navigation preserves in-memory fields; a declined payment/order error does not reset contact, address, method or cart.
  • [ ] T19-AC04 Each page can advance only after its own inputs validate and draft save succeeds. Editing earlier data invalidates quote/intent.
  • [ ] T19-AC05 No guard submits an order or starts payment; all writes require explicit UI actions.

Strict shared acceptance criteria

  • [ ] T19-G01 Read the resolved external mi-bolt-design/SKILL.md and its task-relevant references before implementation AND independently before verification. Record skill version, R01–R26 applicability, files, viewports and evidence in the task review.
  • [ ] T19-G02 Use the resolved latest stable Angular 22.x only; no SSR, prerendering, hydration, NgModule-based application structure, zone.js or legacy structural directives.
  • [ ] T19-G03 For UI/state changes use Angular signals, signal inputs/outputs and built-in template control flow. Consume API data exclusively through the generated SDK and MSW boundary.
  • [ ] T19-G04 All applicable unit acceptance scenarios are automated against deterministic mocks. New shared/browser runtime files must appear in both coverage manifests, including unexecuted files. Node-only targets must appear in their separate 100% unit and black-box command/integration coverage manifests; no silent exclusions.
  • [ ] T19-G05 No live business API, telemetry, remote font, payment, email or SaaS request; no test skip/focus, coverage-ignore comment, threshold reduction or hidden generated-file editing.
  • [ ] T19-G06 Attach fresh command output, task-specific assertions and design evidence. Planned checks are not passing checks. Leave the task unaccepted when any assertion fails.

Evidence record (must be populated by the implementer)

Record source revision, exact dependency versions, skill hash, input scenarios, command and exit status, independent unit metrics, applicable screenshots/viewports, defect list and reviewer decision. This planning package deliberately contains no invented passing metrics or completed-task status.

  • [ ] T24-AC03 Cart badge resets from ordered-cart handling; a new shopping action creates a fresh cart, preserving past receipt replay.
  • [ ] T24-AC04 Browser back to submitting/review cannot resubmit the committed cart; redirect to existing receipt or new-cart action.
  • [ ] T24-AC05 Keyboard focus lands on success heading; no success relies solely on toast or animation.

  • [ ] T24-G03 For UI/state changes use Angular signals, signal inputs/outputs and built-in template control flow. Consume API data exclusively through the generated SDK and MSW boundary.
  • [ ] T24-G04 All applicable unit acceptance scenarios are automated against deterministic mocks. New shared/browser runtime files must appear in both coverage manifests, including unexecuted files. Node-only targets must appear in their separate 100% unit and black-box command/integration coverage manifests; no silent exclusions.
  • [ ] T24-G05 No live business API, telemetry, remote font, payment, email or SaaS request; no test skip/focus, coverage-ignore comment, threshold reduction or hidden generated-file editing.

Independent test/review cycle

  • [ ] Read this packet, dependency outputs, docs/spec/acceptance.md, docs/spec/testing.md, and mi-bolt-design with relevant references.
  • [ ] Add a named failing test for EACH criterion below to the owned spec files, before its implementation. UI criteria require rendered DOM/user-action assertions; API criteria require actual SDK requests intercepted by MSW.
  • [ ] Execute the focused tests and capture a failure caused by the missing behavior, not an import/configuration error. Use the exact scenarios for test groups: release in docs/tasks/test-scenarios.json.
  • [ ] Implement one criterion at a time in the listed ownership boundary. Keep transport, state, presentation and mock domain logic separate.
  • [ ] Run the focused tests, then the project gates documented in docs/spec/testing.md; add new files to both source-coverage inventories, preserving the 100% thresholds.
  • [ ] Independently reload mi-bolt-design and verify relevant rendered states at the stated viewports. Attach evidence for all applicable R01–R26 rules, not a self-reported 'looks good'.
  • [ ] Record actual commands, failures/fixes and final outputs in docs/mi-bolt/execution/tasks/T29.md; commit a single independently reviewable deliverable after acceptance.

  • [ ] T29-AC01 All baseline tasks and project-wide criteria are marked accepted with real evidence; optional 3D remains explicitly out of scope unless approved.
  • [ ] T29-AC02 Independently reload and apply mi-bolt-design to the final build, not only earlier component screenshots.

Strict shared acceptance criteria

  • [ ] T29-G02 Use the resolved latest stable Angular 22.x only; no SSR, prerendering, hydration, NgModule-based application structure, zone.js or legacy structural directives.
  • [ ] T29-G03 For UI/state changes use Angular signals, signal inputs/outputs and built-in template control flow. Consume API data exclusively through the generated SDK and MSW boundary.
  • [ ] T29-G04 All applicable unit acceptance scenarios are automated against deterministic mocks. New shared/browser runtime files must appear in both coverage manifests, including unexecuted files. Node-only targets must appear in their separate 100% unit and black-box command/integration coverage manifests; no silent exclusions.

  • [ ] T29-G06 Attach fresh command output, task-specific assertions and design evidence. Planned checks are not passing checks. Leave the task unaccepted when any assertion fails.

MiBolt MVP · projekt-dokumentáció · 31 feladat / 44 komponens / 16 API-művelet
05 / TESZTELÉS ÉS TELJES ELFOGADÁS

A 100% nem jelző.
Négy konkrét mutató.

Unit: statements, branches, functions és lines. Mindegyik 100%, fájlonként és összesítve.

Unit · 100 / 100 / 100 / 100

Vitest + Angular TestBed + MSW

Minden saját futó kód szerepel a nevezőben: bootstrap, guard, helper, facade, UI és mockdomain. A meg nem látogatott fájl is nulla lefedettséggel része a mérésnek.

A HTTP-t használó teszt a valódi generált SDK-t és HttpClientet hajtja meg; a hálózati oldalt az MSW helyettesíti. Nem az éppen vizsgált üzleti viselkedést mockoljuk ki. [S11–S12]

E2E

Nincs e2e-csomag

E2E: nincs e2e-csomag a termékgazda 2026-09-20-i döntése óta; a megjelenő felületeket a valós böngészős ellenőrzés fedi le (lásd docs/spec/testing.md).

A nevező nem rejthet el saját kódot. A változatlan generált SDK, vendor és fordító által előállított belső kód külön kategória; az SDK minden művelete ettől még szerződés-/transporttesztet kap. Node-only eszközök külön 100%-os unit és parancsszintű integrációs mérést kapnak. HTML/CSS állapotokat DOM-, állapot- és vizuális mátrix bizonyít, nem JavaScript-számláló.

127 tervezett tesztforgatókönyv

124 kötelező alapforgatókönyv és 3 opcionális 3D-csoport. A paraméterezések miatt a tényleges tesztesetszám magasabb lesz. Ez tesztterv, nem már lefutott Angular teszteredmény.

Összes forgatókönyv, művelet és elvárt eredmény
